Pure-Node pixel-diff classified CONTENT-level; VLM reads of both band crops isolated the delta: the product photos are BLANK in the new capture with every text/price/badge element identical (all 25 images verified present on disk; the DB refs unchanged; the parity grid had just verified the live rendering). Root cause: the 02 shot gated only on data-hydrated — a Playwright fullPage screenshot expands the viewport past every loading="lazy" card at once and the raster can outrun the below-fold decodes (run 53's byte-identical 02 won the race; this cold-server run lost it on rows 2-3 while rows 1+4 won — the probabilistic signature of the S35-2/S37-2/S42-2/S50-1 capture-gate family, on the lazy-image surface). Fix: waitForImages(page) in scripts/capture-screenshots.mjs — promote every img to loading="eager" (per spec, flipping a not-yet-loaded lazy image to eager STARTS its load; no scrolling, no layout perturbation) and gate on document.images complete with naturalWidth > 0 (15s timeout — a genuinely broken image fails the capture loudly) + a 120ms decode settle, applied inside shoot() for mode === "full" (ONE insertion point covering every structurally-exposed full-page shot). Verified RED → GREEN: the re-capture ran 25/25 green with 02 back at ZERO changed pixels (byte-identical to the committed raster); the sole remaining delta 03-product-detail at 664 pixels in a single 11px band at y1168-1178 — exactly the documented traffic-owned Date.now() e2e-review-row class.
